WWelcome back to the Draft Viewer! Today we've got a triple *Scars of Mirrodin* draft from the Top 8 of a Pro Tour Qualifier on **Magic Online**. You can find a full Pro Tour Qualifier for Pro Tour–Nagoya [here](https://web.archive.org/web/20110310152153/http://www.wizards.com/Magic/TCG/Article.aspx?x=mtg/tcg/events/ptqmtgo/nagoya/11).

[![](https://media.magic.wizards.com/image_legacy_migration/images/magic/daily/activity/da618_ss.jpg)](https://web.archive.org/web/20110105000000/http://gatherer.wizards.com/magic/draftools/draftviewer.asp?draftid=mtgo1910355)

murphys\_luck

### Champion

**Jeremie Ross, 2-0**
